1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the molecular space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 921 and the convergence rate is O(829/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to molecular theory, we decompose the problem into 921 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 829. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
